# Cookbook notebooks → agent skills: migration record

Where each retired file of `.agents/skills/cookbooks/cosmos3/generator/audiovisual/` went. The sources are the files'
last version on `main` before their retirement (`7738e9e`, after #329). Notebook cells were migrated 1:1 (Jupyter
plumbing and preview/viewer cells aside); everything the skill adds is marked "Skill note (not in the notebook)" or
declared inside the block that carries it.

Renamed on 2026-11-10 (#385): the skill directory `cosmos3-generate-media` is now `cosmos3-inference-generate-media`, so every inference skill carries the `cosmos3-inference-<row>` name; text and `eval/eval.config` unchanged.

| Retired source (`cookbooks/cosmos3/generator/audiovisual/`)                                                                                     | Destination (`.agents/skills/cosmos3-inference-generate-media/`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Notes                                                                                                                 |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `README.md` — introduction, licensing, asset catalogue, per-backend quickstarts                                                                 | `references/payloads.md` § 1–3 (introduction, licensing and access, asset catalogue); the quickstarts in `references/diffusers.md`, `cosmos-framework.md`, `trt-llm.md` and `distilled-trt-llm.md` § "Quickstart (cookbook README)" and `vllm-omni.md` § "Run with vLLM-Omni"; `SKILL.md` Variants 1–6 quote each backend's smoke path | The README's FP8 pointer (#329) is in `payloads.md` § 1                                                               |
| `run_with_diffusers.ipynb`                                                                                                                      | `references/diffusers.md`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | `SKILL.md` Variant 1                                                                                                  |
| `run_with_cosmos_framework.ipynb`                                                                                                               | `references/cosmos-framework.md`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `SKILL.md` Variant 2                                                                                                  |
| `run_with_vllm_omni.ipynb`                                                                                                                      | `references/vllm-omni.md`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | `SKILL.md` Variant 3; follows the notebook's final version (#329: launch mounts, `HF_TOKEN`, "Quantized Checkpoints") |
| `run_with_sglang.ipynb`                                                                                                                         | `references/sglang.md`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `SKILL.md` Variant 4                                                                                                  |
| `run_with_trt_llm.ipynb`                                                                                                                        | `references/trt-llm.md`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `SKILL.md` Variant 5                                                                                                  |
| `run_distilled_with_trt_llm.ipynb`                                                                                                              | `references/distilled-trt-llm.md`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `SKILL.md` Variant 6                                                                                                  |
| `run_with_nim.ipynb` (retired earlier by #296)                                                                                                  | no copy: `SKILL.md` Variant 7 delegates to the NIM cookbook `.agents/skills/cookbooks/cosmos3/nim/` and the `cosmos3-inference-nim` skill (promoted from the cookbook's nested `cosmos3-nim-user` by #384)                                                                                                                             | —                                                                                                                     |
| Content the six notebooks share — asset sets, payload helpers, sampling defaults, resolution table, use-case matrix, per-backend request fields | `references/payloads.md` § 4–11                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | one copy instead of six                                                                                               |
| `assets/` — prompts, negative prompts, reference images                                                                                         | `assets/` (vendored copy)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | The cookbook keeps `assets/`, `distill/` and `finetune/`                                                              |

Links into the retired files: the cookbook READMEs and the docs navigation point at `SKILL.md`; the NVIDIA/cosmos
short links were re-pointed to `SKILL.md` and its vLLM-Omni section (NVIDIA/cosmos#390).

## Visual Reasoning: `cookbooks/cosmos3/reasoner/` → `.agents/skills/cosmos3-inference-reasoning/`

Where each retired file of `.agents/skills/cookbooks/cosmos3/reasoner/` went. The sources are the files' version on
`main` at `bbd4a67`. Notebook cells were migrated 1:1 by a deterministic converter (every markdown and code cell in the
notebook's order; code cells fenced, `%%bash` cells as `bash` with the magic line removed; outputs omitted — the
committed notebooks have none); the README and the prompt guide were copied verbatim. Nothing in the sources was
corrected, re-pinned or reworded: everything the skill adds is marked "Skill note (not in the source)" (one
provenance note at the top of each reference) or lives in `SKILL.md` as a summary that quotes the sources. The only
edits inside `references/` are the link targets listed below.

| Retired source (`cookbooks/cosmos3/reasoner/`)                                                                 | Destination (`.agents/skills/cosmos3-inference-reasoning/`)                                                    | Notes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `README.md` — backend choice (memory footprint), per-backend Quickstart + Notebook walkthrough, NIM quickstart | `references/reasoner-readme.md` (verbatim)                                                                     | `SKILL.md` quotes the memory-footprint figures (Model sizes vs GPU), the OpenAI-client quickstart in its port-8001 ("Run with TensorRT-LLM") form for Variants 2–4, and the Transformers quickstart                                                                                                                  |
| `reasoner_prompt_guide.md`                                                                                     | `references/prompt-guide.md` (verbatim)                                                                        | `SKILL.md` "Prompting the Reasoner"; docs nav "Prompting the Reasoner" points here                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `run_with_cosmos_framework.ipynb`                                                                              | `references/cosmos-framework.md`                                                                               | `SKILL.md` Variant 1 (incl. § 7a Nano FP8)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `run_with_vllm.ipynb`                                                                                          | `references/vllm.md`                                                                                           | `SKILL.md` Variant 2 (incl. "Quantized Checkpoints": Edge / Super FP8)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `run_with_sglang.ipynb`                                                                                        | `references/sglang.md`                                                                                         | `SKILL.md` Variant 3                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `run_with_tensorrt_llm.ipynb`                                                                                  | `references/tensorrt-llm.md`                                                                                   | `SKILL.md` Variant 4                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `run_with_transformers.ipynb`                                                                                  | `references/transformers.md`                                                                                   | `SKILL.md` Variant 5 (incl. § 3a ModelOpt FP8)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `run_with_trt_edge_llm.ipynb`                                                                                  | `references/trt-edge-llm.md`                                                                                   | `SKILL.md` Variant 6                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `assets/` — 15 sample images and clips (git-LFS)                                                               | `assets/` (vendored copies of 12 files, 3.9 MiB, plain blobs; a nested `.gitattributes` exempts them from LFS) | Not vendored (too large): `drive_scene_next_action.mp4` (3.27 MiB), `temporal_localization_1.mp4` (14.0 MiB), `temporal_localization_2.mp4` (3.5 MiB) — they stay in the cookbook's `assets/` as LFS objects; `SKILL.md` "Bundled assets" says how to materialise them. The cookbook keeps `assets/` and `finetune/` |

The NIM quickstart of the README stays inside `references/reasoner-readme.md`; the NIM cookbook pages
(`cookbooks/cosmos3/nim/`) and their nested skill are unchanged. `SKILL.md` points to the quantization skill
(`cosmos3-quantization`) for making an FP8 checkpoint; the released-FP8 inference routes are the notebooks' own
sections, surfaced per variant. That skill is created by the Quantization row of this migration: the `../cosmos3-quantization/SKILL.md` pointers
in `SKILL.md` resolve once that row is merged. Links from the migrated copies to pages that sibling rows retire — the
quantization README (`reasoner-readme.md`, `vllm.md`) and the policy TensorRT-Edge-LLM notebook (`reasoner-readme.md`,
`trt-edge-llm.md`) — point at those pages' current location, so every link resolves at this change's head; they go dead when
the sibling row merges and are re-pointed at that row's skill in the follow-up round (a merge-order dependency between the rows).

Three more edits that are not source text: the converter's provenance note in `cosmos-framework.md`, `sglang.md`,
`transformers.md` and `vllm.md` (notebooks whose first cell is only the SPDX comment) sits after the title cell instead of
before it (rumdl MD041); the repository's `rumdl` pre-commit hook re-aligned the `COSMOS3_UV_GROUP` table of
`cosmos-framework.md` § 4, inserted the blank line between `### Embodied Reasoning` and `#### Robotics Next Action` in
`sglang.md`, `tensorrt-llm.md` and `vllm.md`, inserted the blank line between `## Inference Setup` and
`### Sampling Parameters` in `prompt-guide.md`, and removed a trailing space in `reasoner-readme.md` ("Run with Cosmos
Framework"); and `transformers.md` § 1 is wrapped in an HTML-comment pair (`rumdl-disable MD034` before the section,
`rumdl-enable MD034` after it, plus a marked skill-note comment) so the hook leaves the notebook's bare
`https://astral.sh/uv/0.11.29/install.sh` as written instead of wrapping it in angle brackets.

Link-target rewrites inside `references/` (link text unchanged; a moved file is linked by its new reference name, an
unmoved file by its unchanged location reached from `references/`; the prompt guide's asset links point at this skill's
vendored copies (`../assets/`) and, for the three clips that are not vendored, at the cookbook's LFS copies):

## Quantization (COOKBOOK row "Quantized (FP8)") → `cosmos3-quantization`

Where each retired file of `.agents/skills/cookbooks/cosmos3/quantization/` went. The sources are the files' version on
`main` at `bbd4a67` (2026-11-07). The three notebooks were converted 1:1 (every markdown and code cell in notebook order;
`%%bash` cells as `bash` fences with the magic line removed; the committed notebooks carry no outputs) and the README was
copied whole — including its "Inference", "Mixed activation precision" and "What gets produced" sections, because the
inference skills link to it. The skill is part 2 of the cookbook (how to quantize); part 1 (inference with released FP8
checkpoints) stays in the inference skills' own FP8 sections. Everything the skill adds is marked "Skill note (not in
the source):" or is a summary in `SKILL.md` that quotes the source.

| Retired source (`cookbooks/cosmos3/quantization/`) | Destination (`.agents/skills/cosmos3-quantization/`) | Notes                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| -------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `README.md`                                        | `references/quantization-readme.md`                  | whole README; `SKILL.md` quotes its Setup (once) block, the three Run commands (as text — they executed the retired notebooks), the Demo vs. full runs table, the What gets produced tree and the Inference notes |
| `quantize_cosmos3.ipynb`                           | `references/quantize-cosmos3.md`                     | `SKILL.md` Variant 1 (Edge / Nano / Super); eval case `framework-edge-quantize-fp8`                                                                                                                               |
| `quantize_super_text2image.ipynb`                  | `references/quantize-super-text2image.md`            | `SKILL.md` Variant 2; eval case `framework-super-t2i-quantize-fp8`                                                                                                                                                |
| `quantize_super_image2video.ipynb`                 | `references/quantize-super-image2video.md`           | `SKILL.md` Variant 3; eval case `framework-super-i2v-quantize-fp8`                                                                                                                                                |
| (assets)                                           | —                                                    | the cookbook shipped no assets: nothing vendored, nothing left behind; the calibration prompts and the I2V fallback images are datasets the recipe fetches at run time                                            |

The notebook-derived references keep the notebooks' text; the converter's provenance note sits after each notebook's
first cell (title and introduction). Link targets re-pointed inside `references/` (link text unchanged):

- `quantization-readme.md`: `./quantize_cosmos3.ipynb` → `quantize-cosmos3.md` (2 links), `./quantize_super_text2image.ipynb`
  → `quantize-super-text2image.md` (2), `./quantize_super_image2video.ipynb` → `quantize-super-image2video.md` (2);

## NIM deployment → `cosmos3-inference-nim` (the cookbook's nested skill moves up; the pages stay)

The NIM row of the Inference Cookbook migration has no notebooks: upstream replaced `run_with_nim.ipynb` with the Cosmos3 NIM
cookbook pages and example clients (#296; see the `run_with_nim.ipynb` row above). The cookbook ships its own agent skill in a
hidden folder inside the cookbook directory, which agents started at the repository root do not discover (the repository's
skill roots are `.agents/skills/` and `.claude/skills/`). This row promotes that skill to
`.agents/skills/cosmos3-inference-nim/` (with the `.claude/skills/cosmos3-inference-nim` symlink) and retires the nested copy
(`.agents/skills/cookbooks/cosmos3/nim/.agents/skills/cosmos3-nim-user/SKILL.md`), so one copy exists; the cookbook's
`AGENTS.md`, `cosmos3-inference-generate-media` (three links) and `tools/docs/site/agent_skills.md` point at the promoted skill. The NIM
pages, `AGENTS.md`, `examples/`, the pinned client environment (`pyproject.toml`, `uv.lock`) stay in
`.agents/skills/cookbooks/cosmos3/nim/` and remain the source of truth; the docs navigation keeps its "Deploy with NIM" entry
on the cookbook README. Moving the pages and the example clients into the skill directory is a later housekeeping change
(the clients find the shared assets through their position in the cookbook tree). `SKILL.md` quotes the
README's "Use an Existing NIM Endpoint" and deployment.md's bash blocks (verbatim, in page order) and keeps its longer skill
notes in `references/skill-notes.md` (skill-authored, nothing from a source). Sources: `main` at `bbd4a67`.

Asset vendoring policy: LFS media up to about 1.2 MB were materialised as plain blobs in the entry skill's `assets/` (6,626,544 B in
all, verified against the LFS pointers' sha256), with one exception: `videos/robolab_example_rollout.mp4` (864,271 B), an output
example that no source in this checkout references, was left in the cookbook; larger media were not vendored either and stay as LFS
objects in the cookbook's `assets/`, together with their examples' plain-blob `meta/` and `data/` files. The block skills have no assets of their own and reference the
entry's copies as `../cosmos3-inference-action/assets/…`; the entry's `SKILL.md` documents how to materialise a non-vendored example.

(whitespace only): table alignment including the delimiter rows (the install table of both `cosmos-framework.md`
conversions; the two tables of `action-readme.md`) and one collapsed double blank line after a heading in the
forward-dynamics `cosmos-framework.md` (§ "Stitch and Visualize DROID Generated Video"), `sglang.md` and `vllm-omni.md`
(§ "Visualize AV Generated Videos"). Reverted to the notebook text, with the hook kept off the affected cells by
HTML-comment directive pairs placed between cells (each with a marked skill-note comment; the comments are not source
text): `### Set up HF Cache:` (and, in the title cell of the forward-dynamics notebook, `### Start the SGLang server:`) in
the forward-dynamics, inverse-dynamics and policy `sglang.md` (`rumdl-disable MD001 MD026` / `MD026`), and the bare URL
`https://astral.sh/uv/0.11.29/install.sh` in the forward-dynamics, inverse-dynamics and policy `diffusers.md`
(`rumdl-disable MD034`). The converter wrote its provenance note directly after the SPDX header; where that put it before
the H1, the integrator moved it after the notebook's title cell so the H1 stays the first content line (rumdl MD041).
Every notebook cell is otherwise present verbatim (`verify_md.py`: the only cells it still flags are those carrying a
link-target rewrite from the table above or a whitespace change listed here; fence parity even).

sibling rows retire — the Reasoner TensorRT-Edge-LLM notebook (`action-readme.md`, the policy `trt-edge-llm.md`) and the

## Simulation inference: `cookbooks/cosmos3/simulation/` and the four nested families → `cosmos3-inference-simulation` and three block skills

Where each file of `.agents/skills/cookbooks/cosmos3/simulation/` and each inference file of the four nested skill
families (`cosmos3-nano-transfer-auto`, `cosmos3-nano-sim-auto`, `cosmos3-nano-sim-bimanual`, `cosmos3-nano-sim-transfer`)
went. The sources are the files' version on `main` at `45fecab`. The whole cookbook folder is retired (188 files: 40 outside
the WSM renderer package and the package's 148): its README becomes the reference of the entry skill
`cosmos3-inference-simulation`, and its Auto, Bimanual and Transfer folders become the block skills
`cosmos3-inference-simulation-auto`, `cosmos3-inference-simulation-bimanual` and `cosmos3-inference-simulation-transfer`, which
also take over the inference side of the four families. The rule was verbatim relocation: guides and family files were moved
or copied word for word and the eleven notebooks were converted 1:1 (every markdown and code cell in the notebook's order, code
cells fenced as `python`; the committed notebooks have no outputs and no cell magics). Nothing in the moved text was corrected or
re-pinned. The edits are link targets that pointed at a moved file, path anchors and location text that the move invalidated, marked
skill notes, and the regrouping needed to carry four `SKILL.md` bodies into three skills and to split shared files (heading levels,
16 retitled headings, the sub-skills' frontmatter, nine reference-list items that became table rows, six lines split by sentence,
re-padded tables); all are listed below. The four family folders keep `post-training/`, the training-only parts of
`shared/` and `troubleshooting/` and, for Sim-Transfer, `scripts/paibench_local_bundle.py` with its test. Those files moved in the
change that the next section records, which retires the four family folders.

<!-- rumdl-disable MD060 -->

### Where each source went

Where this section says that a file under `cosmos3-nano-*` stays, says what such a file still holds, or re-points a link to one, it
describes the tree at this change's head, and the path and its line numbers are those of that tree; the next section ("Simulation
post-training") maps each of those files to its present path.

Line numbers in this section are those of the tree at this change's head; source line numbers are those of the file at `45fecab`. A

ignored) finds each of the 41,986 non-blank source lines of the 236 sources that were compared line by line in the tree, except 134
lines changed for a path anchor or location text, 8 frontmatter lines, 16 retitled headings, 9 list items that became table rows and
6 lines that were split by sentence; each of these is in a table below. The other 33 sources, all in the renderer package and

characters when padded are written without column padding, because their cells are whole paths and code lines; the comment pair
around this section switches the table-alignment lint rule off for it.

The four inference `SKILL.md` files. The two Auto bodies were carried into one new `SKILL.md` as its sections `## Transfer-Auto`
and `## Sim-Auto` (headings two levels down, with the model name added where the two bodies had the same title), and three blocks
that did not fit the 500-line limit were moved whole into the overflow reference `references/start-here-and-sampling.md`, each
replaced by a one-line pointer. The Bimanual and Sim-Transfer files were moved to the new skill folder and edited in place: the
numbered workflow sits one level down under `## Cosmos Framework`, the Bimanual token section is the overflow reference
`references/hugging-face-token.md`, and the Sim-Transfer route for a self-trained Stage 3 checkpoint moved to the Sim-Transfer
post-training `SKILL.md` because its block reads variables of the training shell.
What the new files add around the carried text is new: frontmatter, "When to use this skill" (a heading and two bullets, at L21 of
the Auto file, L21 of the Bimanual file and L29 of the Sim-Transfer file), "Paths", "Choose a variant", the prerequisites pointer,
one section per served backend (vLLM-Omni, SGLang), "Check the result", "Troubleshooting", "Adapt the model", the "Reference files"
table and "Related skills"; the Auto file also has "Render WSM controls from your own scene". The served sections of the Auto and
Sim-Transfer files quote the launch block of their reference (four and two `docker run` blocks). The two served sections of the
Bimanual file name the reference sections to run, in order, and quote sentences of the reference; its launch blocks are not quoted
and stay in `references/vllm-omni.md` and `references/sglang.md`. The entry `SKILL.md` is new text that quotes the README copy.

Tests. The six cookbook test files and `prepare_hf_sample_test.py` are in `tests/skills/<skill>/` (rows above). They test the same
behaviour against the new layout: helpers are imported from the skill's `scripts/` folder, and the code cells they used to read from
a notebook are read from the converted reference through the new loader `tests/skills/reference_cells.py`, which returns the
notebook-shaped structure `{"cells": [{"cell_type": ..., "source": [...]}]}` (one code cell per fenced `python` block, the text
between two blocks as one markdown cell). The notebooks' code-cell ids are restored by position where a test looks a cell up by id.
No test was removed and no assertion dropped. Two checks of `test_framework_auto_helpers.py` no longer discriminate and are kept as
written: in `test_checkout_discovery_and_clean_notebooks` the assertions that a code cell has no outputs and no execution count read
the keys with `.get()`, and a Markdown reference carries neither (the loader returns neither key), so they hold by construction; in
`test_vllm_notebook_imports_shared_helpers_in_a_fresh_process` the loop that checked `prepare_lidar_sweep` in
`helpers/lidar_visualization.py` and in the duplicate `auto/lidar_visualization.py` (see "Removed") runs twice on the kept helper.

New files without a source: the entry `cosmos3-inference-simulation/SKILL.md`; `eval/eval.config` in each of the three block
skills; `tests/skills/reference_cells.py`; and the four `.claude/skills/cosmos3-inference-simulation*` symlinks.

### Removed

- `cookbooks/cosmos3/simulation/auto/lidar_visualization.py` (286 lines): byte-identical duplicate of
  `auto/helpers/lidar_visualization.py`, which is kept as `cosmos3-inference-simulation-auto/scripts/helpers/lidar_visualization.py`.
  The one test that imported the duplicate now imports the kept helper.
- The three `agents/openai.yaml` files of the Bimanual family (interface metadata for one agent product; each file held the licence
  header and one `interface:` block). Their content:

| Removed file (`.agents/skills/`)                               | `display_name`                            | `short_description`                             | `default_prompt`                                                                                                       |
| -------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `cosmos3-nano-sim-bimanual/inference/agents/openai.yaml`       | Cosmos3 Nano Sim Bimanual Inference       | Generate and evaluate bimanual future video     | `Use $cosmos3-nano-sim-bimanual-inference to generate or evaluate a single-view forward-dynamics video.`               |
| `cosmos3-nano-sim-bimanual/post-training/agents/openai.yaml`   | Cosmos3 Nano Sim Bimanual Post-Training   | Operate four-stage single-view post-training    | `Use $cosmos3-nano-sim-bimanual-post-training to prepare and run a single-view bimanual training stage.`               |
| `cosmos3-nano-sim-bimanual/troubleshooting/agents/openai.yaml` | Cosmos3 Nano Sim Bimanual Troubleshooting | Diagnose single-view bimanual workflow failures | `Use $cosmos3-nano-sim-bimanual-troubleshooting to diagnose a single-view training, inference, or evaluation failure.` |

- The frontmatter of the four retired inference sub-skills (the new skills have their own; each old description is carried word
  for word, and the last column says where):

| Retired sub-skill file (`.agents/skills/`) | `name` | `description` | Where it went |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `cosmos3-nano-transfer-auto/inference/SKILL.md` | `cosmos3-nano-transfer-auto-inference` | Prepare or run Cosmos3-Nano-Transfer-Auto in Cosmos Framework on camera WSM controls and per-view captions, with optional initial RGB and joint LiDAR. Use for camera mapping, WSM/renderer and synchronization checks, supplied-caption review, av-per-view-caption generation or upsampling, targeted-KPI captions, native requests, sampling settings and output inspection. Input-checking and caption-only requests stop after the requested workflow. | description carried word for word as a paragraph of the new `SKILL.md` (L67); name cited at L19 |
| `cosmos3-nano-sim-auto/inference/SKILL.md` | `cosmos3-nano-sim-auto-inference` | Prepare inputs or run Cosmos3-Nano-Sim-Auto camera-only or joint camera/LiDAR generation with a released or exported student in Cosmos Framework. Check causal conditioning, per-camera captions, rolling metadata and saved outputs. | description carried word for word as a paragraph of the new `SKILL.md` (L237); name cited at L19 |
| `cosmos3-nano-sim-bimanual/inference/SKILL.md` | `cosmos3-nano-sim-bimanual-inference` | Generate action-conditioned video with Cosmos3 Nano Sim Bimanual in Cosmos Framework. Download the matched release, prepare RGB and action inputs, run inference, inspect outputs, or use an exported student from the user's Stage 3 training. | description carried word for word as the opening sentences of the new description; name not carried |
| `cosmos3-nano-sim-transfer/inference/SKILL.md` | `cosmos3-nano-sim-transfer-inference` | Generate RGB video with a matched Cosmos3-Nano-Sim-Transfer checkpoint. Use HF example2 for ordinary spatial-control inference and example1 for long-rollout depth or segmentation inference. | description carried word for word as a paragraph of the new `SKILL.md` (L22-23); name not carried |

Nothing else was dropped. The source lines that no longer exist as written are the frontmatter lines above; the headings in the
retitled-headings table (`## References` became `## Reference files` in all four bodies, with the Bimanual and Sim-Transfer lists
kept under it and the Auto lists turned into table rows); two items of the Sim-Auto reference list that were word-identical to
Transfer-Auto items and are kept once; the lines changed for a path or a location (next section); six lines of the Bimanual
`release-workflow.md` that were split by sentence (split table above); and the argument `"$AUTO"/*.py` of one lint command in
`validate-cookbooks.yml`, which matched only the removed duplicate.

### Path anchors and location text changed in code and references

One row per change (134 recorded changes: 107 in the tables of this section, 26 under "Infrastructure", and 1 entry that only
records that the two moved Bimanual validator scripts needed no change), plus two command lines in `docs/`. A value in code type is
a line, or part of a line, of the file (old: at `45fecab`; new: at this change's head); plain text describes a change that is not a
single line, such as added lines or padding.
In the skills, in the family files that stay and in the tests under `tests/`, every changed code line carries a
`# Skill note (not in the source): ...` comment on the line above; where the line is a continuation of a command, or one of several
lines that were changed or added together, the comment is above the command or the block. Changed location text (a path in prose, a
table cell, a JSON example) has a skill note in the same section that quotes the old text. Recorded only in these tables, without a
note in the file: the three path lines of the renderer package (`scripts/wsm/README.md` L26-27 and
`scripts/wsm/cosmos_auto/lidar/cli.py` L42; the package carries no skill notes); the two framework tests and the configuration files
under "Infrastructure"; and the two command lines in `docs/`. The six rows whose old and new value is a heading record a heading
that is one level down in an assembled reference; a level change has no note of its own.

two that pointed at the old `#run` end in `#run-transfer-auto`; the one in `docs/inference_sim_auto.md` that pointed at the old
Sim-Auto `#start-here` goes to `references/start-here-and-sampling.md#start-here-sim-auto`, where that block is; the two backticked
paths in `cosmos3-inference-transfer/SKILL.md` name the new `SKILL.md` without an anchor); the three links to the Bimanual
environment page that meant inference prerequisites end in `SKILL.md#prerequisites`, and the one in the Bimanual
`references/evaluation.md` keeps that page, `cosmos3-nano-sim-bimanual/shared/release-workflow.md`, as its target, because the
training install that the scoring entry point needs stays there; the three links from the Sim-Transfer training pages that meant
inference from a trained checkpoint end in `#run-inference-from-your-trained-checkpoint` (the "Inference" cell of the starting-point
table in the post-training `SKILL.md` goes to the inference `SKILL.md`). The site's "Simulation" page is now the entry `SKILL.md`;
the README copy is reached from it. The two guides that the docs site holds back stay held at their new paths.

### Skill notes added

Every addition inside moved text is marked `Skill note (not in the source)`. There are three forms: a provenance note after the first
heading of a converted, copied or assembled reference (21); a note in prose, as a blockquote, where a split or a changed location
needs a pointer (40); and a code comment on the line above a changed or added code line (118, of which 58 in the tests). Text that is
new as a whole (the four `SKILL.md` frontmatters and their added sections, the entry router, organising headings such as
`## Sim-Auto`, `## Support request` and `## Inference action sidecar`) is not marked line by line; the tables above say which lines
are carried.

### Infrastructure

- `.gitattributes`: the Git LFS rule for the renderer's example scene archives and the exemption of `docs/camera-rig/preview.png`
  follow the package to `scripts/wsm/`.
- `.github/workflows/validate-wsm.yml`: the two `paths` filters and `WSM_DIR` name the new package folder; nothing else changed.
- `.github/workflows/validate-cookbooks.yml`: the path filters also cover `.agents/skills/cosmos3-inference-simulation*/**` and
  `tests/skills/**`; `AUTO` and `BIMANUAL` are the skill folders, with `AUTO_TESTS`, `BIMANUAL_TESTS` and `TRANSFER` added; the test
  steps run from `tests/skills/`. One step is new, "Lint notebook code in converted skill references": it rebuilds each of the eleven
  converted references as a notebook from its fenced `python` blocks and lints it with the rule set of "Lint notebook code". The JSON
  parse and notebook-schema steps no longer apply to the eleven notebooks, which are Markdown now.
- `.pytest.toml`: the comment of the `wsm` entry of `norecursedirs` names the new parent folder (the entry matches by directory name).
- `.pre-commit-config.yaml`: the top-level `exclude` gains the new package folder (it was covered by the `cookbooks` alternative), and
  the `uv-lock` hook's exclude names the package's `pyproject.toml` at its new path.
- Two framework tests and one repository test read skill files by path:
  `cosmos_framework/model/generator/tokenizers/lidar/preprocessing_test.py` (renderer and helpers),
  `cosmos_framework/inference/transfer_shipped_specs_test.py` (Sim-Transfer sample script and defaults file) and
  `tests/bimanual_vllm_notebook_test.py` (the Bimanual vLLM-Omni notebook, now read from its converted reference through the loader).
- `tools/docs/nav.yml`: the "Simulation" entry stages `skills/cosmos3-inference-simulation/SKILL.md`, and the two hold entries name
  `references/transfer-auto-guide.md` and `references/transfer-guide.md` (counted under "Link targets re-pointed").

### Not vendored

No media was added to the skills. The samples (the Auto daytime scene, the Bimanual examples, the Sim-Transfer `example1` and
`example2` inputs) are downloaded from the models' Hugging Face repositories by the documented workflows, as in the cookbook; the
request files under `assets/daytime_scene/` hold paths relative to the downloaded sample. The renderer's example scene (the 12
archives under `scripts/wsm/examples/rds_hq/`) and 9 documentation images under `scripts/wsm/docs/` stay Git LFS objects; their
pointer files moved as pointers.

### Known and deliberately unchanged

Defects of the sources that were carried word for word (not corrected in this change):

- Auto guide (`references/transfer-auto-guide.md`): the native command omits `--resolution`, `--num-steps` and
  `--per-view-negative-prompt`; the order of the output listing; the phrase "described in its inference guide".
- Auto README copy and Simulation README copy: the FlashDreams GitHub link has no `tree/<branch>` segment.
- `references/sim-auto-sglang.md`: the export flag `--cosmos3-nano-sim-auto` and its configuration key.
- Auto `SKILL.md`: the two native run blocks use the sample request with paragraph captions while the notebooks default to
  `captions_with_timestamp`.
- Sim-Transfer guide and notebooks: SGLang is missing from "Supported backends"; the guide's vLLM-Omni launch passes
  `--no-guardrails` where the notebook sets `"guardrails":false`; "every five seconds" against `POLL_INTERVAL = 10`;
  `emphasize_control_in_prompt=True`; the history values 30 and 3.
- Bimanual `references/prompt-guide.md` reads examples from `$CHECKPOINT_DIR/examples/` while `SKILL.md` downloads them to
  `$HF_EXAMPLE_DIR`.
- Bimanual `SKILL.md` names `examples/inference/bimanual_frustum.json`, a path inside the model's Hugging Face repository, in
  backticks (L315, carried from L278 of the old inference `SKILL.md`). The evaluator's static check reads it as a missing repository
  path; it reports nothing else for the three block skills and scores them 95 (Bimanual), 100 (Auto) and 100 (Sim-Transfer). It scores
  the entry skill 85 because a router has no `eval/eval.config`.
- Served launch blocks (quoted in the Auto and Sim-Transfer `SKILL.md`, named in the Bimanual `SKILL.md`): all eight run
  `docker run` in the foreground, the four vLLM-Omni ones with `--rm`; the SGLang blocks of the Auto and Sim-Transfer skills carry the
  placeholder `/absolute/path/to/cosmos-framework/.agents/skills`; the Sim-Transfer vLLM-Omni launch is three blocks whose third
  reads `COSMOS3_REPO` exported by the second; the Sim-Transfer SGLang reference plays the downloaded videos without a frame-count
  or decode check.
- Simulation README checklist, point 1 ("`sample_outputs.json` reports `status: success`"), quoted in the entry `SKILL.md` and applied
  by the "Check the result" section of each block skill: the Transfer-Auto SGLang reference writes `sample_outputs.json` without a
  `status` field, and the Sim-Transfer and Bimanual SGLang references write no `sample_outputs.json` (their record is the job JSON
  saved beside each video).
- Wording that names retired things stays where it is narration or link text: "notebook", "cookbook" and "cookbook root"
  (meaning `.agents/skills`), print labels, variable names (`SIMULATION_ROOT`, `COOKBOOK_ROOT`, `COSMOS_COOKBOOK_ROOT`), link texts
  such as `generate_wsm.ipynb` and `cosmos3-nano-transfer-auto-inference`, the titles `# cosmos3-nano-transfer-auto` and "Caption
  preparation for cosmos3-nano-transfer-auto", and helper names written as `helpers/...py` in the Auto guide.
- The frontmatter descriptions of the four old troubleshooting sub-skills still list the inference symptoms that moved.
- `docs/inference_transfer.md` reads four request files under `$SIM_TRANSFER_ROOT/specs/` that did not exist at `45fecab` and do not
  exist now; only its link target and the variable were re-pointed.
- `scripts/wsm/PROVENANCE.json` and `LIDAR_PROVENANCE.json` record hashes of an upstream snapshot that already differed from the
  package at `45fecab`; they were not refreshed.

Also unchanged on purpose:

- The renderer package contains its own nested skill, `scripts/wsm/skills/scenario-to-rds-hq/` (with `evals/`). It moved with the
  package, byte for byte, and has no `.claude/skills` entry; the docs site's skills table, which lists every `SKILL.md` under
  `.agents/skills/`, lists it under its new path as it listed it under the old one.
- Open pull request #359 adds a note on blur presets to the old Sim-Transfer inference `SKILL.md` together with a code change. The
  code is not on `main`, so the note is not carried.
- Restated material was moved, not merged: text that says the same thing in other words exists in both the guide and the notebook
  references (for example the vLLM-Omni server start).

<!-- rumdl-enable MD060 -->

## Simulation post-training: the four nested families → `cosmos3-post-train-simulation` and three block skills

Where each file that the previous section left in the four nested skill families (`cosmos3-nano-transfer-auto`,
`cosmos3-nano-sim-auto`, `cosmos3-nano-sim-bimanual`, `cosmos3-nano-sim-transfer`) went. The sources are those 47 files as that
change left them: the text of `main` at `45fecab`, with the splits, the re-pointed link targets and the skill notes that the
previous section records. The four family folders are retired. 38 files moved as files: 30 references, the Sim-Bimanual and
Sim-Transfer post-training `SKILL.md`, three scripts and three tests (the tests to
`tests/skills/cosmos3-post-train-simulation-transfer/`). The other nine (the two Auto post-training `SKILL.md` and the
troubleshooting files of the four families) were assembled into six files: the `SKILL.md` of `cosmos3-post-train-simulation-auto`
with two overflow references, and one `references/troubleshooting.md` in each block skill. The block skills
`cosmos3-post-train-simulation-auto`, `cosmos3-post-train-simulation-bimanual` and `cosmos3-post-train-simulation-transfer` hold
everything the families held, and `cosmos3-post-train-simulation` is a new entry skill that routes to them. The rule was verbatim
relocation, as in the previous section: nothing in the moved text was corrected. The edits are link targets (169 in the moved and
assembled files, 105 in files edited in place), the script path in five training commands and in the three tests, 30 heading levels
and 10 heading titles, the frontmatter of the eight sub-skills that end here, the two Auto reference lists that became table rows,
skill notes, and re-padded tables; all are listed below.

<!-- rumdl-disable MD060 -->

### Where each source went

Line numbers of the tree are those at this change's head; source line numbers are those of the file as the previous section left it.
Paths are relative to `.agents/skills/` unless they start with `tests/`, `docs/` or `tools/` or name a file of the repository root.
A line-by-line comparison of the 41 Markdown sources with the tree (padding, heading level and link targets ignored) finds each of
their 3384 non-blank lines, except 32 frontmatter lines, 10 headings whose title changed, five command lines whose script path
changed, 9 skill notes of the previous section that were reworded, five skill notes that a provenance note replaces, and the 18
lines of the two Auto reference lists (two headings, 16 items); each of these is in a table below. The three scripts are
byte-identical at their new path. In the three tests, 1 line of the sources is replaced and 21 lines are new (blank lines counted).
Tables that would be wider than 340 characters when padded are written without column padding; the comment pair around this section
switches the table-alignment lint rule off for it.

### The old `SKILL.md` bodies

The two Auto bodies are the sections `## Fine-tune Transfer-Auto` and `## Distill Sim-Auto` of one `SKILL.md` (475 lines). Their
headings are one level down; the two titles that the bodies shared ("Choose your starting point", "Set up the environment") carry
the model name; and the two old titles are replaced by the section titles. Five blocks that did not fit the 500-line limit moved
whole into the overflow references `references/transfer-auto-training-notes.md` and `references/sim-auto-training-notes.md` (five
sections; the runs are in the table above), each behind a one-line pointer in `SKILL.md` (four pointers, because two of the Sim-Auto
blocks followed each other). The Sim-Auto part still opens with the rule that the student starts from a Transfer-Auto checkpoint
(L241-244, source L8-11).

The Sim-Bimanual and Sim-Transfer bodies were edited in place (329 and 319 lines). In both, "Set up the environment" is one level
down under a new `## Prerequisites`, and `## References` is `## Reference files` with the source list kept under it. In the
Sim-Bimanual body one block changed place: "Activate the Framework environment" (source L37-57) closes "Prerequisites" (L53-73)
instead of opening "Quickstart: train Stage 0". No block of the Sim-Transfer body or of the two Auto bodies changed its order. The
section "Run inference from your trained checkpoint", which the previous section moved into the Sim-Transfer body, is carried with
it.

### Infrastructure and tests

- No workflow or configuration file changed: none of the 9 files under `.github/workflows/`, `.github/skill-eval.yml`,
  `.pytest.toml`, `.pre-commit-config.yaml` or `.gitattributes` names an old family, a post-train skill or one of the moved tests.
- `.claude/skills/` gains four symlinks, each to `../../.agents/skills/<name>`: `cosmos3-post-train-simulation`,
  `cosmos3-post-train-simulation-auto`, `cosmos3-post-train-simulation-bimanual` and `cosmos3-post-train-simulation-transfer`. The
  old families had none.
- The three tests moved with the scripts they test. No CI job ran them before this change and none runs them now: no workflow names
  them, and a repository-wide `pytest` run collects only files named `*_test.py` (`.pytest.toml`), which these are not. Run by hand
  they pass: `test_nano_conversion.py` 16 tests (standard library only), `test_paibench_local_bundle.py` 3 tests (with the Framework
  importable) and `test_qa_transfer_stage.py` 5 tests (with `hydra` importable, as in the Framework environment).
- Three triggers differ for a change limited to these tests, and no job runs the three tests either way: `validate-cookbooks.yml`
  starts, because its path filter holds `tests/skills/**`; the push trigger of `docs.yml` no longer starts, because its path filter
  holds `.agents/skills/**` and nothing under `tests/` (on a pull request that workflow has no path filter); and the changed-path
  check of `gpu-tests.yml`, which exempts `.agents/*` and `*.md`, no longer exempts them, so its GPU jobs run.
- The skill evaluation selects a skill by its `eval/eval.config` (`.github/scripts/skill_eval/run_matrix.py`), and
  `.github/skill-eval.yml` names none of the skills of this change: the three block skills each have a config and the entry skill, a
  router, has none.
- `.agents/skills/` held 31 `SKILL.md` files before this change and holds 27: the eight sub-skill files are gone and the four
  post-train skills are new. The docs site's skills table is built from the `SKILL.md` files under `.agents/skills/`
  (`tools/docs/stage.py`) and follows.

| Test                                                                                | Source                                                                      | Test methods | CI job before | CI job now |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------ | ------------- | ---------- |
| `tests/skills/cosmos3-post-train-simulation-transfer/test_nano_conversion.py`       | `cosmos3-nano-sim-transfer/post-training/scripts/test_nano_conversion.py`   | 16           | none          | none       |
| `tests/skills/cosmos3-post-train-simulation-transfer/test_paibench_local_bundle.py` | `cosmos3-nano-sim-transfer/scripts/test_paibench_local_bundle.py`           | 3            | none          | none       |

### Eval drafts

Each block skill has a drafted `eval/eval.config`: 10 cases in all (Auto 4, Sim-Bimanual 3, Sim-Transfer 3). None was run. The three
files have the same top level: `credentials` [`HF_TOKEN`], `gpus` 1, and a budget of 90 minutes and 3,000,000 tokens that is not
measured. No case launches training: 8 of the 10 prompts say so in words, `auto-bundle-timestamped-captions` asks only for a
training bundle and `nano-conversion-preflight` only for the converter's environment check; each case stops at a bundle build, a
data check, a conversion or a dry run. Every case has the assertion that the documented workflow ran without errors on the first
attempt.

### Known and deliberately unchanged

Defects and oddities of the sources that were carried word for word (not corrected in this change):

- Auto `SKILL.md`: the Sim-Auto Quickstart has four numbered steps and its step 3 says "in step 5" (L347); the Transfer-Auto
  Quickstart converts the released checkpoint with `prepare_sim_auto_checkpoint` (L108), a name the source explains at L115-116. The
  sentence at L99-100 sends the reader to "Model snapshots" in the inference skill's `references/framework-runtime.md` for the RGB
  VAE and the text tokenizer; the previous section split that page, and the lines that name the two sources are now under "Training
  components" in `references/runtime.md` (L46-47 and L50-51), which the "Prerequisites" paragraph (L37) quotes.
  `references/sim-auto-training-notes.md` L52 says "MR". `references/troubleshooting.md` keeps the model identifier as a heading
  (L18).
- Sim-Bimanual: `references/multinode-launcher.md` L13 says "Replace only the main tutorial's `TRAIN` array", and `SKILL.md` has no
  such array. `SKILL.md` gives the launch of Stages 1 to 3 as prose ("Repeat the config check and launch with this TOML", L249), not
  as commands. `SKILL.md` L172 exports `IMAGINAIRE_OUTPUT_ROOT` from `BIMANUAL_OUTPUT_ROOT`, a variable that no file sets.
- Sim-Transfer: `SKILL.md` L59 calls `SIM_TRANSFER_SKILL` "the absolute location of this skill bundle", while the setup reference of
  the inference skill sets it to the inference skill folder (`cosmos3-inference-simulation-transfer/references/setup.md` L23; the
  note at L64 of `SKILL.md` says so). The guard `: "${SIM_TRANSFER_SKILL:?Set the absolute skill bundle location}"` stays in three
  blocks (`SKILL.md` L77, `references/local-training.md` L25 and `references/nano-conversion.md` L20), although no command of the
  skill expands the variable. `references/environment.md` L5 says that Nano conversion keeps a separately pinned checkout, and
  `references/nano-conversion.md` L12 says that no separate checkout is needed. `references/local-dataset.md` reads
  `SIM_TRANSFER_OUTPUT` (first at L19), which the inference skill's `references/hf-sample.md` sets (L30).
  `references/troubleshooting.md` has two headings titled "Training state" (L9 and L43); the anchor `#training-state`, which the
  inference skill links, is the first. The sources have no export step for Sim-Transfer, so the skill has no export section.

Also unchanged on purpose:

- Restated material was moved, not merged. Examples: the 14 lines from L332 of the Auto `SKILL.md` are also L31-44 of its
  `references/sim-auto-data.md`; the Transfer-Auto download command is in both parts of that `SKILL.md` (L105 and L295); the 15
  lines from L127 of the Sim-Transfer `SKILL.md` are also L31-45 of its `references/local-training.md`; and the `mujoco==3.10.0`
  requirement is in the Sim-Bimanual `SKILL.md` (L69) and in its `references/environment.md` (L17).
- Outside this record, 24 lines in 12 files of the two Auto skills still contain an old family name. None is a path that must
  resolve: 5 are skill notes of the previous section that quote the former location of the negative prompt ("this was …"), 2 name
  the retired inference sub-skills (a provenance sentence and a link text of the source), and the others are the lower-case model
  identifier in carried text, in the export flag `--cosmos3-nano-sim-auto`, in headings and in the anchors built from headings (for
  example `#troubleshoot-cosmos3-nano-transfer-auto`).
- Link text in the docs is unchanged: `README.md` L83 and L84 still say "Transfer-Auto training skill" and "Sim-Auto training skill"
  and now point at the two sections of one skill. The two post-training entries of `tools/docs/nav.yml` (L64 and L67) stay links to
  the file on `main`, as before; the navigation has no entry for the post-train entry skill. The hand-written skills table of
  `README.md` (L152-159) is untouched: it has 6 rows and none for a Simulation skill.
- The evaluator's static check scores the three post-train block skills 100 and reports nothing for them. It scores the two entry
  skills 85, because a router has no `eval/eval.config`, and the inference skills 100 (Auto), 95 (Sim-Bimanual, the backticked
  Hugging Face path named in the previous section) and 100 (Sim-Transfer), the scores that the previous section records.
- The descriptions of the four old troubleshooting sub-skills are dropped with their frontmatter ("Removed text"); their wording is
  in no `description` of the new skills.
